(1) T030o51 (O-018): inside a finding, in both readers (the manifest pass, and the canonical pass in a canonical round before its completion marker), T030o48's narrowed grammar gains three reasons, each read in every reading `vf_cm_scan` makes of the line (no open span, or a span an earlier line of the run left open, read as an opener and as literal text): a `<` followed by a digit or a symbol an email address's local part may begin with (CommonMark 0.31.2 6.5: one of `.#$%&'*+=^_{|}~-` or a backtick; a letter, `/`, `!` and `?` keep naming raw HTML) outside every code span (`an autolink outside a code span in finding <ID>`); an inline link's destination or title, or a full reference link's label, read from a `]` followed by `(` or `[` that some reading places outside every code span, by 6.3's productions (`vf_cm_dest`: an optional space, then a pointy destination or a bare one with balanced parentheses and backslash escapes, then an optional title in double quotes, single quotes or parentheses, then `)`; `vf_cm_label`: up to the first unescaped `]`, none at an unescaped `[`), that holds a backtick (`a backtick inside a link destination, title or label in finding <ID>`), or that has not ended when the line does, since a destination may begin on the next line and a title or a label may span lines (`a link destination, title or label that does not end on its line in finding <ID>`); a `](` or `][` that every reading places inside a code span is literal, which keeps the round-17 report's own quotations parsing. And the round's minor: the narrowed check is judged after the line's own heading has opened or closed a finding, so a heading that opens a finding is judged under it, the first of a round included, a deeper heading and a report title under the finding they stay in, and a heading that closes a finding under none, every other line as before. (2) T030o52 (O-024): inside a finding, in both readers, a line is refused by name when it opens a code fence (three or more backticks or tildes) after indentation or a list marker, anywhere but at the start of the line (`a code fence that may be inside a list item in finding <ID>`; 4.5 and 5.2: the shared tracker opens a fence only at the start of a line and at most three spaces in, and a renderer ends a fence a list item opens with the item, so the tracker can miss such a fence, or open and close it out of phase with the renderer, as the sibling the invoker derived shows, which the reviewer's M2 does not reach), and when its text, after indentation, list markers and block-quote markers, begins a link reference definition (a bracket closed at once by `]:`), or closes by `]:` a label that a bracket at the start of an earlier line of its run left open (`a link reference definition in finding <ID>`; 4.7: a definition renders nothing while its title may carry a field line, and a label may span lines); the run reader carries an open label to the next line of its run and drops it with the run. Session 4 repaired all four as recorded in its Decisions entry and passed locally at 143/143; the focused review of that delta is pending.
- [ ] **Harness-level bash startup environment (recorded 2026-09-24, NOT repaired, user decision pending):** every captured command runs as `bash -o pipefail -c`, which reads `BASH_ENV`, `ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS` and exported functions at startup, so a file outside the repository can decide any captured verdict with the identity unchanged. This reopens the justification of the 2026-09-13 environment rule. The options put to the user were to hash bash's startup inputs into the identity, or to pin a closed harness environment, each as its own plan-level task. Round 11 may challenge this.
- [ ] **Analyzer field grammar (observation, not repaired, user's call):** `sdd-analyze.sh` reads a field from any line carrying its marker, both ways (Decisions, 2026-09-24). With it, AC-023's loop now certifies the identity's view of 001/002 rather than their raw bytes; the reviewers flag that as a narrowing that needs either an AC-023/plan wording decision or the grammar fix.
- [x] *Superseded: O-015 was repaired as T030o41 and credited RESOLVED by round 11; kept for the record.* **O-015 (round 10, blocking, wrong-result, not repaired).** The O-010 guard in reuse-checked `bash tests/run.sh` reads raw bytes, while the identity hashes `vf_semantic_file` output, which drops Evidence lines and `status:`/`updated:` frontmatter. A flagged sentence on those lines makes the suite fail while the floor stays current and the PASS stays reusable. There is also a latent half: enumeration without `-z` skips git-quoted filenames. The reviewer's proposed follow-up is T030o41, which scans semantic content with NUL enumeration, fails on a git error, and carries fixtures in both directions. **Repair needs the user's decision.** Until then, do not tick T030 or write any Evidence/frontmatter text that states the superseded environment membership. The reviewer notes a signed waiver as the alternative and recommends repair.
